                Young people first: safeguarding and safety in Scouting
              </h2><p class="lead mb-0">
                Wherever we go and whatever we do, we put young people’s
                safety and wellbeing first.
                <a class="" href="/what-scouts-do/stay-safe/">Here's how</a>.
